# Reference parallel C — source reading and selected live states

> 2026-10-09 QR 정리: 원시 URL 원장·캡처는 현재 보관하지 않는다. 과거의 원장/이미지 보존 문구는 당시 작업 기록이며, 현재 확인 가능한 결과·실패·미확인 범위는 이 문서 본문이다. 새 조사나 재검증을 수행한 것은 아니다.

- Date: 2026-10-07. Scope: Component Gallery, Uiverse, 3dicons, Refero.
2026-10-07 scope update: maximum OS accessibility text and expansion simulating that maximum are excluded from design/implementation judgment, new tests, verification, follow-ups, completion and release blockers under the user-updated root AGENTS.md. Earlier performed observations remain historical only. Earlier pending maximum-text mentions are superseded by this exclusion and do not create active work.

- This is research evidence, not implementation, adoption, release, or exhaustive interaction QA.
- Existing captures were preserved. Only this report, its own reading index, and three proof PNGs were added. No shared source/ledger, package, lock, dist, Git, remote CI, or native runtime was changed.
- Reading means actually reading captured text; HTTP 200, inventory, and screenshots do not count as reading or interaction completion. Captured sources may differ from the current site.

## Refero: why extracted tokens cannot be adopted unchanged

[Wise style detail](https://styles.refero.design/style/367c0c6e-73a7-441c-a8ff-91d139ac60dc) places #9fe870 lime in conflicting roles. Its color-role prose/export says “Do not promote it to the primary CTA color”; its Guidelines recommend lime for primary-action fills and active controls. The description and segmented-control prose also use lime for active states. The page’s no-drop-shadow guidance conflicts with its Elevation recipes, including 10px/32px and 40px/40px card shadows. The observation is an inconsistency within Refero’s generated recommendations, not a finding about the original Wise product.

[Cursor style detail](https://styles.refero.design/style/4e3b4717-84c8-4599-baaf-a343c3d619b6) repeatedly exports invalid five-digit #26251 in prose/CSS examples while the palette’s Ink value is #26251e. Guidance about avoiding pill treatment conflicts with pill/button descriptions. Do not mechanically turn these exports into registered presets. Each role requires validation against HJM semantic intent and real rendered states.

Both pages distinguish normalized measurement, interpreted roles, and reconstructed HTML from original source components. Their image previews and component reconstructions provide visual direction; they do not establish original keyboard, responsive, loading, or business behavior. Original Wise/Cursor sites were not exercised here. Wise DesignTokens initial section and DESIGN.md major sections were read; remaining JSON and other export tabs remain pending. Cursor major DESIGN.md sections were read; the full quick-start/export set remains pending.

The 52 general pages consistently put product purpose and actual content first. Useful shared additions are usage guidance for display/reading/technical typography roles, section density/rhythm, screenshot crop/frame/mobile alternatives, and how a composition selects quiet versus operational presentation. Product requirements still own transaction rules, commercial prices, data, permissions, and state transitions. A proposed DESIGN.md specification and vendor-history assertions were read as page claims, not independently verified standards.

## 3dicons: selected live interaction chain

[Explore](https://3dicons.co/explore): clay + front radio state selected; visible image currentSrc paths and complete images matched front/clay. Typing Notebook alone did not update results; Enter returned one result. [Notebook detail](https://3dicons.co/icons/628100-notebook?color=clay) opened as a Dialog over Explore. Gradient + iso changed the real image to the 400px gradient isometric asset after an initial loading placeholder. The placeholder was transient, not a persistent defect.

Download opened a menu for current icon, angle bundles, .fbx, .blend, and all icons; no file was downloaded. Pressing Escape on the download trigger closed both the menu and the detail dialog, restoring the prior Notebook search with clay/front. This is observed nested Escape propagation; its code cause was not isolated. Popular sort was selected with one result, so ordering correctness was not demonstrated. Searching `no-such-icon-qa` with Enter displayed No icon to display and preserved clay/front.

Absorb through existing Asset/Image, Dialog/Menu, SearchField/EmptyState, and finite collection composition. A theme can select a material/angle bundle while products own asset meaning and license mapping. Decorative 3D illustrations should not replace functional Icon semantics or official provider assets. Preserve a loading fallback and image layout stability.

캡처 설명(원시 이미지 정리): 3dicons no-result state. 관찰 결과와 검증 한계는 이 문서의 본문에 보존한다.

Proof PNG SHA-256: `1d9338bbe3f0882df5cae6c62c345ddcf9df1e29714f39a7b947d13a2af6ffe1`.

## Component Gallery: one linked contract read, not complete original behavior

[Carousel page](https://component.gallery/components/carousel) has 22 examples. React filter showed 10, clear filters restored 22, name sort persisted. Gallery global search accepted Carousel by ArrowDown/Enter. The theme menu used System/Light/Dark menuitemradio; Dark changed the site canvas while static example images retained their original appearance. System was restored.

[Cedar Filmstrip](https://cedar.rei.com/components/filmstrip) full body was read: purpose, applicability, anatomy, interaction, content, accessibility responsibility, adapter/model/frame implementation, events, API. It uses five parts, finite related items, partially visible frames to communicate overflow, and owner-provided data adapters. Whole-frame navigation and nested actions need distinct focus handling. The page says Storybook documentation is not available for this component; no working Filmstrip demo was exercised.

Current HJM web Carousel is a single active finite panel; inactive panels are hidden/inert. Cedar’s multiple-visible scroll model is therefore a distinct collection presentation contract, not a safe cosmetic theme switch. A finite filmstrip/peek composition is a candidate for an explicit presentation capability with focus, scroll, and announcement contracts. It should not silently change Carousel semantics.

## Uiverse: reuse a treatment, retain the Button contract

[3D-buttons category](https://uiverse.io/ui/3d-buttons) and [thin-owl-11](https://uiverse.io/njesenberger/thin-owl-11) were read. The post has a native button plus top/bottom/base decorative layers. CSS uses inset shading, radial gradients, 200ms transitions, and a 6px active translation. Space/focus appearance was observed; an application action result or a held pressed frame was not verified. The inspected CSS has no explicit disabled/loading or prefers-reduced-motion handling; this is a source observation, not complete accessibility QA.

The useful addition is an optional press-feedback treatment on the existing HJM Button engine, with disabled/pending, keyboard, reduced motion and native equivalents preserved. Do not add a second functional Button simply to copy this skin. HJM profiles currently expose contentTransition and selectionMotion; press feedback can be assessed as a separate capability. The post’s MIT notice was read; substantial copied code would require its attribution. No code was copied or installed.

## Outcome and follow-up

This pass reduces Refero’s captured general-body pending denominator from 52 to zero, without marking visual/behavior completeness. The owned JSON contains one URL/hash/finding for every completed general page. Fixed sorted style detail reading is next; any further completion must record an actually read body, not a keyword match. Whole-site completion still requires remaining bodies and real UI/interaction coverage. Research judgments above are proposals; source implementation changes remain parent-owned.

## Ordered style-body reading checkpoint

Full captured text, including canonical DESIGN.md, generated CSS/Tailwind quick starts and similar-card labels, was read for the first three hundred and seventeen lexically sorted style URLs. This is 317/1,342 style bodies; complete visual and behavioral review of these three hundred and seventeen remains pending. The owned index records each hash and reading judgment.

- [pampam.city](https://styles.refero.design/style/001480cb-05f4-4802-be39-84b942169481): paper canvas, display serif and tilted browser-frame composition. Periwinkle role prohibits primary CTA while Guidelines and reconstructed Button prescribe it; the agent guide also says no distinct CTA. Nineties fallback is sans in quick-start CSS despite a serif substitute recommendation. OpenType features are described but absent from quick-start recipes. This strengthens the case for role validation rather than raw preset imports.
- [Getburnt](https://styles.refero.design/style/002cc5a7-0d34-4d8d-afa0-c5fad69477d5): body text description uses 1.5 line height at 16px, while CSS exports `--leading-body: 24` and other large unitless leading values. Direct use as CSS line-height would mean a multiplier, not a pixel height; no rendered failure was tested. Captured 1440px pill radius is a shape choice rather than a new common spacing/radius scale. Monochrome recommendations must not suppress semantic error/status accessibility.
- [David Kirschberg](https://styles.refero.design/style/004f4856-4b01-4c23-a9fb-866303d5013b): dark gallery, two surface levels and shadowless thumbnails; imagery supplies color. The floating navigation and horizontal gallery are composition capabilities. Never-grid and single-weight rules are this reference’s recommendation, not a global HJM requirement.

## Live provenance mismatch: labelled TeePublic

The fourteenth sorted [Refero detail](https://styles.refero.design/style/0231caf5-0347-4f2d-ba20-5bab8fcaf2ce) is titled TeePublic throughout its description and DESIGN.md, but the origin link is https://unfurproject.com. The current live page reproduces that title/link mismatch. Its visible preview contains unrelated lottery promotion imagery; this is not verified evidence of the original TeePublic brand design. No original-domain browsing, registration, transaction or external mutation was performed. This is an observed provenance problem within the reference record, not a claim about the cause or current state of TeePublic itself.

Exclude the record from official-brand implementation evidence until its origin/capture/identity are reconciled. Its body also recommends no large violet fills while describing full-width violet promo/trust bands, and its red role differs from urgent-state recommendations. Those source recommendations were read, but should not enter a preset without validation.

Its 14px weight-400 Bone White-on-Coral action pair gives 2.966:1 by independent opaque-sRGB relative-luminance calculation, below ordinary small-text contrast; this is static source-pair validation, not an original rendered-site audit. Preserve semantic foreground checks rather than copying that pair. Serif Vivey substitutes are Playfair/DM Serif/Lora but quick-start falls through sans. Pill-only tag/product-frame rules conflict with rectangular sticker and square shop-card imagery; distinguish geometry by purpose. Headline leading 1–1.15 conflicts with smaller heading scale 1.2/1.33. Announcement single-line directive must adapt to translation/zoom. Shop dropdown, category active state, cart, bundle, subscription, newsletter, marquee pause and mobile behavior remain pending.

106. [Arva design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/15846be3-8df8-42e4-a05c-d9395dcec369) — Pastoral forest presentation: cream canvas, forest structural bands, lime marquee, full-height agricultural-photo hero and a three-card alternating pastel quilt row; serif editorial headings with sans functional type. Quilt colors are content presentation variants, not selected/success/error semantics. Hero explicitly has white text over a photo without overlay, so crop-specific foreground contrast needs original verification rather than a universal no-scrim rule. Typography summary uses 0.025em/-0.022em/-0.012em tracking but scale/CSS export re-labels the same numerals px; display -2.96px correctly matches 80px × -.037em and does not validate the other units. Reckless serif substitutes conflict with sans quick-start fallback. Every-button 100px radius conflicts with the 110px header CTA and 9999px guide; geometry must resolve with responsive size rather than literal universal radii. Bone #f1efdf canvas differs from quilt bone #efefef (Ash Gray), preserve distinct surfaces. Marquee motion/pause, photo crop, hero dynamic viewport, nav dropdown/language, forms and mobile quilt collapse remain unverified; imagery is not evidence of a procedural paper/forest texture.

107. [Swap design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/15d10d6c-1844-46c6-ae69-c99a56e6ad41) — Mint editorial greenhouse: text-driven hero, light serif display with lime-to-citrus gradient clipped to glyphs, pill paired actions, white soft-shadow cards and five-column logo grid. Actual logo rights/content remain product-owned. Gradient text and lime focus border need semantic contrast separate from black-on-lime CTA: independent opaque-sRGB checks of the three prescribed gradient stops on Mint Wash #f2ffe3 are 1.216/1.176/1.078:1, below the ordinary large-text threshold; this is a reconstruction color-pair calculation, not original rendering proof. A low-contrast decorative gradient must not replace readable heading content. Custom-serif placeholder has a serif fallback while detected secondaryFont exports sans; resolve actual face mapping/weight support instead of copying unresolved aliases. Gradient-only-display minimum 72px conflicts with no-below-48 rule and 48–72 section-headline recipe. CSS has gradient while Tailwind omits it; ss01 feature is prose-only, and 80-120px/8-16px custom-property ranges are not directly usable CSS lengths. Guideline weight100 hero/features differs from300 section recipes; make typography roles explicit. Forms/focus, nav, logo-grid collapse, responsive long headings and reduced-motion remain unverified.

## Experiment registration candidates — pending, C369

User requested registration after research (2026-10-07). The following is the manual mapping of all candidate classes found in the 52 general + 317 detail readings and selected Component Gallery/Uiverse/3dicons review. It is a proposed experiment backlog; no experiment was registered here. Each own-index page carries candidate IDs, and each catalog entry preserves every assigned source URL. The same treatment found on many pages has one candidate. Source errors become validation fixtures, not copied product tokens. Further unread pages may add candidates.

The proposed path is a Storybook hierarchy string, not a created file. Existing API references are verified read-only against current source; public additions and actual registration belong to root. Paths were corrected against [Storybook navigation §1.1–1.3](../STORYBOOK_NAVIGATION.md): exactly four segments, existing fixed category vocabulary, unique Korean item names of at most 13 characters (limit 16). Current literal story titles were read for name collisions; dynamic titles and actual registration must still pass the normal registration checks. Collapsible maps to data display per the contract catalog. S06/G01 are comparison compositions, not complete screen flows. No new category was added.

Sequel fill10%는 현 material.surface fillOpacity .85–1과 다름: 접근성/불투명 대안 포함해 실험 검토, 원본 복사 금지; Jeton blur20–40px/fill10%도 현 기본 투명도 계약 밖 비교 후보이며 출처 copy/brain 설명 혼입은 보류 C242: Juicebox 명시적 점 노이즈, Gigantic 포장·어두운 영역의 하프톤/낟알, New Genre 다중 스톱·비대칭 radial은 비교 변형 후보. 밀도·tile·mask·opacity·원본 자산·권리 미확인. 종이·용암·심해 비유만으로 재질을 만들지 않음. C262: Opennote 약40px ruled-line, Adora 반복 유화 자산, DrawHistory 강한 사진 blur와 amp/전자재료 CTA glow를 서로 다른 비교 변형으로 묶음. 원본 asset/밀도/rights/native calibration 미확인. C282: Vana ASCII glyph-density 지도 장식, Shelby 식물 사진10–15% opacity, Schema 기하학 벽화, Recess 구름10–20%·pastel gradient, Yung 흰 선 그림을 각각 장식/이미지 변형으로 분리. 실제 tile·asset·stops·rights·동작·native host는 미확인. C302: 암실·종이·린넨·벨벳·백스테이지·고산 은행의 비유는 실제 grain/fiber와 구분. Dot-matrix/pixel-mapped 사진과 데이터 오류 범위는 asset/data 역할을 따로 보존; glass 8/12px와 불명 alpha를 기존 calibrated host 기본값으로 복사하지 않음. C322: Parallel의 30% white pseudo-data/dithered footer, Browserbase dot/halftone pixel sky, Craft organic flower, Podia flat papercut는 각각 asset/geometry/decorative 역할; 실제 데이터·질감 kernel·모션 미확인. Glass 4/9–20/15px와 opaque export를 공통 기본값으로 복사하지 않음. C342: Aaply 20px dot-grid는 dot radius/alpha 미제공; Hyperliquid organic 3–4 blob과 Ameba edge-dense particle sphere는 geometry/blur/runtime 미제공. C222 checkpoint: 222/1,394 captured bodies read = 52 general + 170 style details; 1,172 style details remain. All 41 candidate classes remain proposed-not-registered. Phantom’s intentionally low-contrast helper text is not an accessibility exemption. Jeton’s collective-wisdom/brain recipe has unresolved provenance and is excluded from product defaults pending original-source comparison.

Root-owned 3dicons follow-up is separate from this captured-body reading count: Tick five loaded variants (gradient/front selected only), Ghost color/front and clay/dynamic with a 390px dark/reduced-motion view, and two Threads assets with front/dynamic. Keyboard download-menu Escape focus restoration and a missing radio-group label target were checked by root. Asset profile-radius inheritance was repaired and checked separately by root; these results do not complete every original icon combination or Native device review.

## Captured detail body batch 171–190 — C242

Twenty full captured bodies were manually read in lexical URL order, including source metadata, canonical DESIGN.md, generated CSS/Tailwind and similar labels. Exact repeated blocks in Midjourney were shown once with original-line references; no unique source content was omitted. Source SHA refers to raw captured HTML, not interpreted CSS. Original pixels, source implementation and complete state matrices remain pending.

Root-owned 3dicons Tick/Ghost/Threads selected styles and Asset profile-radius fix are separate live/implementation evidence; all icon combinations and native actual-device coverage remain incomplete.

C282 checkpoint: 282/1,394 captured bodies read = 52 general + 230 style details; 1,112 style bodies remain. All 41 candidates remain proposed-not-registered. Static opaque sRGB recipes: MotherDuck White/Sky=1.938:1 versus Charcoal/Sky=6.051:1; Forner Pebble/Bone=1.366:1; Mural808080/White=3.949:1; Recess Slate/Periwinkle=1.769:1; BaseHub Bone/Orange=2.558:1 versus303030/Orange=4.649:1; Huddle Honey/White=1.826:1. These low-contrast small-text recipes are excluded from direct absorption. Varo White/Violet=4.749:1 and Andercore White/Red=4.557:1 pass only the static ordinary-text recipe floor. Shelby Olive/Linen=9.166:1 and Sage/Linen=3.698:1 agree with the source rounded9.2/3.7 claim; they do not prove10px legibility. No calculation is original rendered QA. Source role/export conflicts remain reconstruction exceptions, not original-product defects. The ASCII/low-opacity-photo/cloud/line-art variants extend T06/C03 without adding a new map API or assuming real behavior. Root-owned3dicons and Assetprofile-radius evidence remains separate; native actual-device and alloriginalvariants stay incomplete.

No original product flow or new live viewport was exercised in this batch. Repeated patterns extend existing classes; Hume AI adds a proposed rank/error-range display class C14. All 42 candidate classes remain proposed-not-registered.

C322 checkpoint: 322/1,394 captured bodies read = 52 general + 270 style details; 1,072 style bodies remain. All 42 candidates remain proposed-not-registered. Static opaque sRGB checks, not original rendered proof: Fonts In Use Ash999999/White=2.849:1 contradicts its 7.4:1 AAA claim; Charlie Ash838383/Black=5.539:1 contradicts its claimed 3.8:1 and reversed large-text rule. Active Theory Black/Violet343755=1.824:1 and Black/Black=1.000:1 exclude literal small-button recipes. Readwise White/Pen478CD0=3.541:1 and White/OrangeFB9100=2.295:1 exclude ordinary CTA/badge text; GIC Signal41A1CF/White=2.907:1 excludes the 15px outlined label and functional border recipe. Going Lime0BFF80/ParchmentFFFEF0=1.324:1 excludes even large informative emphasis. Relate Slate374151/FCFCFC=10.047:1 contradicts the alleged 17.8:1 minimum. Becklyn Smoke606060/Obsidian1A1A1A=2.768:1 and Smoke/LiftedCharcoal3B3B3B=1.781:1 exclude meaningful inactive navigation and card metadata. Front White/B375F4=3.103:1, White/D0C6F0=1.613:1: one solid endpoint fails even the large 20px label floor, and the actual gradient/alpha composite remains unverified. Tracky CoralFF5858/ButterFEDF89=2.376:1 excludes the 14px announcement link. Walden StoneD3CEC5/White=1.567:1 is insufficient as the sole functional focus boundary. Netflix White/E50914=4.794:1 passes only the opaque recipe text floor; Charlie White/ED1C24=4.382:1 excludes its 14px credit option, though large display has a different threshold. These source recipe conflicts do not establish original-product defects. 3dicons root-owned selected variants and Asset shape/profile regressions remain separate; native actual-device, all original combinations and all pending visual/state reviews are incomplete.

C342 checkpoint: 342/1,394 captured bodies read = 52 general + 290 style details; 1,052 style bodies remain. All 42 candidates remain proposed-not-registered. Static opaque sRGB calculations are recipe checks, not original rendered proof: Kobu location919191/F9F5F2=2.907:1 excludes ordinary 12px labels; Aaply Black/E6E51E=15.591:1 supports its 15.6 claim, but White/F34646=3.636:1 excludes 13px annotations. Kofi focusAAC9F7/White=1.693:1 cannot be the sole focus boundary. Fruitful Forest0B7443/White=5.844:1 and Cycle Iron727578/White=4.635:1 pass their opaque ordinary-text pair only. beehiiv White/MagentaFF5EC4=2.745:1 excludes the 18px normal CTA even at this single opaque endpoint; real gradient composites still need rendered sampling. Peloton footer65666A/181A1D=3.041:1 excludes 14px text. Wise Pebble868685/White=3.644:1 excludes 16px body, and White/Lime9FE870=1.474:1 excludes the palette button-text role while the canonical dark-text role is separate. Hyperliquid White/Mint97FCD7=1.219:1 and Midnight0F3933/Forest072724=1.248:1 exclude the contradictory text roles; dark-text-on-mint nav is a different pair. Synthesia White/8098F9=2.700:1 excludes small CTA hover text, while White/0D0F2C=18.720:1 supports the stated 18.7 dark-panel pair. ATMOS White/6B6CE0=4.333:1 fails meaningful 10px orbital copy but decorative aria-hidden text has a different purpose. Bajgart 878787/White=3.592:1, Ameba Fog6B6B83/00052E=3.822:1, Wiza Ash9491A1/White=3.077:1 exclude ordinary informative metadata. Apple White/0071E3=4.697:1 passes opaque purchase text; Link0066CC/Black=3.772:1 does not support ordinary small dark-stage links without a distinct inverse token. These source conflicts do not establish original-product defects. Root-owned 3dicons selected variants and Asset shape/profile regressions remain separate; native actual-device, all original combinations and all pending visual/state reviews remain incomplete.

## Captured detail body batch 291–310 — C362

All 20 complete captured bodies were manually read before the interruption, including palettes, independent font summaries, canonical DESIGN.md, CSS/Tailwind quick starts and similar-card labels. Findings below are restored from that actual reading record after the temporary tool store reset, without recounting them as a second review. Truncated source measurements remain gaps. No new original-product viewport or interaction was exercised. The same 42 candidate classes remain proposed-not-registered. Maximum accessibility text and simulated maximum expansion are excluded from this batch and all active follow-ups.

291. Filled teal and navy actions have distinct route roles. No-white-canvas rule versus white feature wrappers is context-dependent. Hand-drawn faces/plants and macOS mockups are assets, not CSS paper grain. Sofia 12×.043=.516→.52 and 14×.043=.602 are valid label tracking, while body tracking0 is separate. Mono13×.059=.767 versus card13×.038=.494 conflict;64 preview1.16 versus scale1.2 and50 reverse; P22 italic versus upright metrics and aliases need actual font manifests/licensing. Filter remove X needs a named independent action, not decorative glyph; checklist checkmarks do not establish Checkbox selection. 6 million/1 hour/98% are unverified product claims. ## Final scoped decision — research closed, exhaustive audit false

2026-10-07 user scope change: “이제 조사 마무리해”, “필요한것만 조사해”. Sequential full-corpus reading stops here because the user now requests only evidence necessary to make application choices. The saved boundary is **369/1,394 Refero bodies read = 52 general + 317 style; 1,025 style bodies unread**. No new live original-product visual or flow checks were performed in this closing increment. Earlier selected gallery, Uiverse and 3dicons checks remain limited to their recorded states. This closes this assigned research task; it does not claim exhaustive review of four sites, actual experiment registration, implementation, publication or native validation.

Current public API inspection supersedes earlier speculative API labels in historical findings: TextAnnotation is a Web experimental source file, currently absent from package exports/public map and Native; FeedScreen/Post/ConversationThread/MediaPlayer/MediaLibraryScreen public APIs were not found. Tooltip was found in Web root/overlays, but not the Native public map. Image is Web root/display and Native root/data-display. These gaps remain explicit.

The 42 source classes remain proposed-not-registered. No candidate is discarded merely to reduce the count: reuse absorbs existing contracts, improvement preserves an actual missing expression/role, new experiment preserves a distinct composition/screen contract, deferred preserves data or identity gaps without inventing a product need. Existing APIs, exact source URLs, reasons and essential confirmation conditions are structured per candidate in the owned ledger.

| ID | Final disposition | Reason / remaining essential confirmation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| T01 | improve-existing | ui/code 두 family로 display·reading·small-caps 역할을 독립 전파할 수 없음. family 역할과 문맥 tracking을 보강하되 브랜드 font를 번들하지 않음. 필수: display/reading 역할을 Heading·Text와 양 renderer에 연결; 허용 font·fallback·실제 glyph/로딩 확인 |
| T02 | improve-existing | 유효 profile 검증은 있지만 외부 추출값의 단위·출처·역할 충돌을 정규화하는 경로는 별도다. 자료 fixture와 변환 전 검증을 기존 규격에 흡수. 필수: invalid 값·단위·원본URL/role provenance fixture; 정상 unitless leading2.5와 범위 밖 gradient stop 오탐 금지 |
| T03 | reuse-existing | 브랜드 악센트와 danger/success/data의 의미를 기존 semantic palette로 분리 가능. 출처의 역할 모순은 채택하지 않음. 필수: 기능 foreground/background 쌍과 실제 focus·selected 대비 |
| T04 | reuse-existing | 역할별 radius와 Asset shape가 있으며 의미 있는 circle/pill geometry는 보존. 원시9999px scale 추가 불필요. 필수: control/card/asset 역할별 기존 토큰 상속 확인 |
| T05 | reuse-existing | 기존 spacing·layout으로 section rhythm과 내부 밀도를 비교 가능. 측정된 off-grid 수치를 전역 규칙으로 만들 이유 없음. 필수: 모바일·번역·DOM 순서와 surface별 밀도 비교 |
| T06 | improve-existing | grain/noise·blur/fill·inset은 기존 계약에 있음. 원자료의 hard offset, soft mockup, 여러 inset 역할이 동일 전역 depth로 손실되지 않도록 역할별 적용을 보강. 필수: 실제 recipe가 있는 역할만 비교; Web/Native host 비용·불투명 대안·대비; 사진 재질을 CSS로 추론 금지 |
| C01 | reuse-existing | 장식 press layer·arrow/hover는 기존 Button 행동 위 표현. 입력 엔진 복제 불필요. 필수: focus/pressed/disabled/pending·RTL·reduced motion |
| C02 | improve-existing | 의미 Heading·Text는 재사용 가능. TextAnnotation은 현재 Web 실험 소스만 있고 공개/Native 경로 없음. 부분 장식과 원형 text-path는 실제 gap이므로 공개 경계와 양 renderer 규격을 보강하는 후보. 필수: 실험 소스를 소비 API로 안내하지 않음·Native 구현/공개 사용 지침 필요; 읽기 가능한 DOM·forced colors·자산 실패·정적 대안; orbit/path 원본 미확인 |
| C03 | improve-existing | 현재 shape/meaning·기본 crop을 재사용하며 반응형 crop·scrim과 제공되는3D variant manifest 선택을 비교 보강. 신규 Image 복제 불필요. 필수: 제품 asset manifest/CC0·alt·crop·실패/placeholder; 실제 합성 프레임 대비; 전체3D 변형 미검증 |
| C04 | reuse-existing | 장식 drift/wave는 현 모션·정적 fallback 정책을 유지해서 표현 가능. 필수: 장식과 실제 재생 행동 분리·pause/static fallback |
| C05 | reuse-existing | underline/pill/inset 표현은 동일 입력·오류·복구 계약을 유지. 새로운 field 엔진 불필요. 필수: label/IME/focus/error 설명·pending·실패 후 입력 유지 |
| C06 | reuse-existing | technical typography와 metric/status를 existing 표시 API로 비교 가능. 테마가 live 데이터를 발명하지 않음. 필수: copy·syntax 의미 대비·실제 수치/단위·읽기 대안 |
| C07 | improve-existing | 공개 Banner 확인 안 됨. 기존 표면/행동으로 static notice를 구성하되 sticky 위치·닫기·복구 규격 보강; cookie/promo/membership 목적은 분리. 필수: safe area·이름 있는 닫기·초점 복귀; 동의 저장·수락/거부·계정 가입은 제품 소유 |
| C08 | reuse-existing | center/pill/floating presentation을 기존 navigation 상태와 연결. 새 탐색 엔진 불필요. 필수: current/expanded/Escape/RTL/mobile; hover-only 금지 |
| C09 | reuse-existing | 상품 값·옵션 선택과 panel navigation을 목적에 맞게 기존 선택 계약으로 표현. 필수: selected/focus/keyboard/disabled/unavailable; 재고/SKU 제품 소유 |
| P01 | new-experiment | Carousel는 단일 active·inactive hidden/inert다. 여러 항목이 함께 보이는 finite scroll/peek의 focus/scroll/announce는 고유 구성 gap. 필수: visible 항목 전체 focus·whole-frame/nested action 분리; 끝/페이지 이동·scroll·announce·RTL; 실제 Cedar demo 없음 |
| P02 | improve-existing | 혼합 aspect·비정형 배치는 현 Grid를 재사용하고 profile rows/cards/grid에서 실제 editorial recipe 전파를 보강. 필수: DOM/읽기/초점 순서·동적 높이·모바일 breakpoint |
| P03 | reuse-existing | 본문/이미지 분할을 existing layout과 공개 슬롯으로 구성 가능. 필수: 모바일 읽기 순서·crop·대비·번역 |
| P04 | reuse-existing | 한 화면 mixed surface는 global theme toggle과 별도. 기존 semantic surface를 활용. 필수: 각 밝고 어두운 band foreground·기능 대비 |
| P05 | reuse-existing | tilt/overlap mockup은 장식 asset/layout 조합이며 새로운 실제 editor/chat 기능이 아님. 필수: 좁은 폭 대체 crop·pointer/clip/읽기 순서·asset rights |
| P06 | reuse-existing | pastel card rhythm은 palette/surface·geometry 순서로 표현 가능. 필수: 색/shape 외 분류 label; status 색 오용 금지 |
| P07 | improve-existing | display와 marginal caption 표현은 기존 Heading. responsive 포스터와 title/logo asset 구별을 사용 지침에 보강. 필수: 중요 text clipping 금지·반응형 줄바꿈·권리 |
| P08 | improve-existing | shell은 기존 API. feed/보조 rail의 독립 overflow와 좁은 화면 collapse는 현재 overview 선택만으로 보장되지 않아 구성 규격 보강. 필수: 논리 지역 순서·모바일 collapse·sticky 도구/키보드 overlap |
| P09 | reuse-existing | logo/quote/rating row는 기존 표시 API로 조합. 필수: 실제 logo·인용·실적 권리/근거; 정적 marquee 대안 |
| P10 | reuse-existing | 독립 like/comment/share 행동 row는 기존 버튼. 행 전체 클릭으로 행동 의미 합치지 않음. 필수: pressed/count/pending/error·이름/개별 focus |
| P11 | reuse-existing | specimen/content font는 UI 테마 전파와 별도 data role로 표현 가능. 필수: glyph·필터·설치·font 권리 실제 flow 미검증 |
| S01 | reuse-existing | hero/증거/features/quote/next action은 기존 화면 지역·구성으로 비교. 원제품 marketing 기능을 보장하지 않음. 필수: 제품 목적/필수 행동·모바일·실제 콘텐츠 |
| S02 | reuse-existing | 목록/detail fixture는 existing 표시·선택으로 가능. ProductCatalog 전용 API가 있다는 주장은 하지 않음. 필수: 가격/옵션/장바구니/거래 product 계약·미구현 흐름 표시 |
| S03 | reuse-existing | 전시 화면은 existing layout·asset를 재사용; P01/P02/P12 고유 gap은 해당 후보로 분리. 필수: 권리/목적·locale/menu·모바일; 출처 불일치14번 제외 |
| S04 | reuse-existing | 라이브러리의 shell/geometry/선택은 existing. MediaPlayer/MediaLibraryScreen 공개 확인 못 함; actual 재생은 제품과 별도, finite rail은P01. 필수: named playback·pause/loading/error·search/계정 product flow |
| S05 | reuse-existing | dashboard/editorial/landscape는 기존 profile·OverviewScreen 선택으로 비교 가능. 필수: 정상/로딩/빈 값/오류 fixture·실데이터 표기 구분 |
| S06 | improve-existing | neutral+10named presets 및 custom extends가 이미 있다. 새 preset registry 복제 없이 지원 축이 component→composition→screen까지 전파하는 기존 실험을 보강. 필수: 앱 custom profile 동일 fixture·전환 시 초안/선택 보존; 실제 지원 축만 선택; product theme의 기능 자동 발명 금지 |
| G01 | reuse-existing | purpose/source/state/실데이터 구별과 근거표는 기존 지침·QA에 흡수. 새로운 화면 API가 아님. 필수: source독해/캡처/실제flow 분리; 상태 fixture·불채택 근거 |
| P12 | new-experiment | 세로 브랜드 벽은 같은 Grid/Link를 쓰지만 회전·stacked text와 readable mobile 목록 전환의 고유 구성 recipe 필요. 필수: DOM/읽기 순서·링크 이름·모바일 수평 대안·실제 scroll |
| S07 | reuse-existing | 인증 화면의 hero/main/footer와 고정 카드 pending 계약이 존재. theme는 canvas/hero만, provider 자산/색과 이름-only 버튼 유지. 필수: 제품 provider·동의·로고·권리·실제 인증은 제품 검증 |
| C10 | deferred | ToastDescriptor에 identity asset slot은 없다. 브랜드 그림이 실제 알림에 필요한 제품 사례를 확인하기 전 새 asset slot은 보류; 고정 floating card를 Toast로 오인하지 않음. 필수: 제품의 실제 identity+tone 사용처; timer/pause/action/close/announce 유지 |
| C11 | reuse-existing | hairline FAQ와 plus→cross 장식은 동일 disclosure open/trigger/panel 계약. 필수: cross는 새 닫기 행동 아님; keyboard/focus/reduced motion |
| P13 | new-experiment | 생성 prompt+tool/model 선택+추천 시작점은 MessageComposer 이름 변경으로 충족 안 됨. 기존 입력/선택을 이용하는 목적 구성 추가. 필수: 빈 값·IME·submit/pending/error/복구·초안 보존; 비용/권한/모델/실제 결과 product 소유 |
| S08 | new-experiment | FeedScreen/Post/ConversationThread 공개 export는 현재 대응표에서 확인되지 않는다. 기존 댓글/신고/작성과 feed 읽기·독립 참여 상태를 잇는 목적 화면은 실제 gap. 필수: 읽기·비로그인·pending/error/empty/media fail·모바일 keyboard; 추천/인증/신고/차단/구독은 product 소유 |
| C12 | deferred | 정적 세계 지도+읽기 목록은 재사용 가능하나 Geo/Map 전용 API는 없다. 좌표·투영·선택·데이터 제품 요구가 미확인이라 API/SDK 추가 보류. 필수: 제품 지리 데이터 요구·경계/투영/권리·대체 읽기 목록 |
| C13 | deferred | 비율 donut/ring 전용 API는 없고 Progress는 진행 의미다. 분포 요구와 동일 데이터 대체 목록 확인 전 차트 API/SDK 보류. 필수: 기간/통화/분모/0/음수/누락·계열 이름·읽기 대안 |
| C14 | deferred | 순위 막대·수치 축·오차 범위와 동일 데이터 표는 공개 gap. 통계/원데이터/사용 목적이 미확인이라Progress/C13로 축소하지 않고 보류. 필수: 실제 값·오차 정의·정렬·축/단위·읽을 표·접근 가능한 선택 |

### First apply: maximum five

1. **S06 — 실험/구성/비교와 검증/프리셋과 앱 테마 전파**: 기존 10개 named preset(+neutral)과 custom profile를 확장 지점으로 사용. 같은 콘텐츠·초안·선택을 유지하며 현재 component→composition→screen 전파 비교를 보강; 새 registry나 중복 테마 스토리를 만들지 않음.

2. **T02 — 실험/토큰/편집 도구/외부 값 정규화**: Refero 재구성값을 제품 토큰으로 옮기기 전에 잘못된 hex/단위/범위/역할을 격리. xAI8행간 px→unitless, Suno/Raycast 잘못된5자리 hex는 재현 fixture; B—Line2.5 unitless leading과 VITURE 범위 밖 gradient stop은 유효 대조 fixture.

3. **T01 — 실험/토큰/색과 글자/표시·읽기·기술 글자**: 현재 ui/code만 있으므로 표시 글꼴·읽기 글꼴을 독립 선택하는 실제 gap을 보강. 프리셋과 앱 custom profile에 역할을 전파하고 font 파일/라이선스는 제품 소유.

4. **T06 — 실험/토큰/표면과 움직임/역할별 표면 깊이**: 기존 grain/noise/blur/fill/inset 재질에 역할별 비교를 보강. paper·retro·forest·brutalist·glass·clay에서 차이가 의미 있게 보이도록 하되 사진의 나무/직물/금속을 전역 procedural token으로 추론하지 않음.

5. **P01 — 실험/구성/정보 표시/여러 항목 필름스트립**: 한 active slide의 Carousel 계약을 유지하면서 finite multi-visible filmstrip/peek 구성 실험을 추가. 읽힌 Cedar 문서가 고유 focus/scroll 계약 근거이며 실제 Cedar demo는 미확인.


### Meaningful support axes for the ten themes

Ten named presets already exist in the contract: retro, paper, forest, minimal, editorial, brutalist, glass, aurora, terminal and clay; neutral is additional. Their shared capability set should cover independent display/reading/UI/code families, semantic action/status roles, role-specific corner/crop/asset variants, calibrated material/depth, explicit collection/layout presentation and state-preserving interaction presets. Theme switching may choose supported presentation for a screen purpose; it must not create commerce/social/generation behavior. P13 input+tool choice, S08 feed and P12 vertical brand wall remain distinct new composition/screen experiments outside the first five. C12–C14 data visualization and C10 identity-in-Toast remain deferred with their actual API gaps preserved.

Invalid source exports are quarantined by URL, unit and intended role. Five-digit hex, prose ranges, missing alpha/stops and physical leading exported as unitless numbers must not become palette/default token values by guessing. Valid large numerical unitless leading, negative shadow offsets/spreads and gradient positions outside0–100 need context, not blanket rejection. Contradictory or mismatched origin records (including TeePublic detail14) remain excluded from official-brand implementation evidence. This is a reference reconstruction finding, not an original product defect.

Only necessary future implementation checks apply: chosen public contract and both renderer boundaries, actual source/asset rights where used, meaningful foreground/boundary contrast on actual composed frames, keyboard/RTL/mobile/failure/draft recovery and reduced-motion/static fallback. Maximum OS accessibility text and expansion simulating that maximum are fully out of scope under the current user AGENTS; earlier measurements remain historical and do not create work or block completion/release.

Final disposition counts: {"improve-existing": 10, "reuse-existing": 24, "new-experiment": 4, "deferred": 4}. Registration remains pending with root.
